# Ported-bug register (PIDASHCONV-824)

Every Django bug that was ported to Rust on purpose, so a later
"difference" between the backends is never ambiguous.

## Method

- Sources: all 620 merged PRs into `rust-dev` (bodies read for a
  ported-bug section) plus every "Ported bugs" module-doc section under
  `rust-api/crates/` (630 snippets in 301 files).
- 2081 candidate rows were synthesized and deduped to 2007
  distinct bugs (a PR section and a doc section often describe the same
  bug; the merged row keeps the longest description and every source).
- PRs marked with a dagger (†) did not list the bug in their body; the
  porting PR was attributed with `git blame` on the doc line plus a
  diff check that the blamed commit added the ported-bug text
  (406 rows).
- Every `pin` naming a `contract-tests/` or `fixtures/` path was verified
  to exist in the tree (file or fixture id); unverifiable claims were
  downgraded to `unpinned` with a note.
- `unpinned` (971 rows) means no contract test or fixture was found
  that pins the behavior; the pinning note says how it could be pinned.
- "After switchover: keep / fix" is the maintainer's decision; left empty.
- No bug is fixed here. Do not fix a bug by editing this file.
